儒鴻(1476 TT) Trading Buy

營運穩定成長，長期展望正向。 
投資建議：預估儒鴻 2026 年營收為 408.71 億元，YoY+7.59%，
稅後 EPS 24.79 元。2027 年預期在產能稼動維持高檔及客戶訂
單穩定下，營收成長至 424.43 億元，YoY+3.84%，稅 後 EPS 25.52
元。考量：(1)3Q26 毛利率如預期的順利回升，預期未來幾個季
度的毛利率可維持現有水準。(2)持續投入自動化設備更新提高
生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投
產，推動長線的成長動能。綜上所述，對儒鴻的投資建議為
Trading Buy，目標價 385 元 (2027 年 PER 15.5X)。  
預估 4Q26 營收維持成長，稅後 EPS 6.09 元：儒鴻 3Q26 營收
維持高檔為 105.72 億元，YoY+9.74%， QoQ+4.82%，毛利率亦
受惠客戶新產品出貨增加，產品組合優化及匯率相對友善等因
素，順利提升至 31.18%，稅後 EPS 為 6.36 元，符合預期。預期
4Q26 由於成衣價格上漲且客戶新品訂單持續出貨，可推動儒鴻
營收維持成長，預估儒鴻 4Q26 營收為 105.93 億元，
YoY+14.34%，QoQ+0.20%，毛利率 31.23%，稅後 EPS 6.09 元。 
美國期中選舉結束，市場不確定因素消除，儒鴻訂單能見度維
持六個月左右：美國期中選舉在民主黨獲勝的結果下，可預期
極端的關稅政策不會再發生，不確定性因素逐漸消除，不過目
前也沒看到品牌端有追加訂單的動作出現，預期未來幾個月的
訂單及終端銷售會處於正常的水準。目前儒鴻的訂單能見度維
持在六個月左右，市場庫存也在相對健康水準，整體市況穩定。 
儒鴻持續投入自動化設備更新提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因
應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產：儒鴻持續擴增新產能及
自動化設備更新以因應客戶需求，除了在台灣、越南二地的生
產設備持續更新外，印尼廠新產能也計劃在 4Q27 產能投產。目
前印尼廠的產能利用率維持滿載水準，印尼廠的生產需求未來
的成長空間仍大，預期等印尼廠三期投產後，可為儒鴻帶來下
一段成長的動能。

### Page 2

個股報告 
2026 年 11 月 11 日

2 群益投顧 
  儒鴻的 ESG：  
儒鴻 2016 年即制定溫室氣體盤查管理程序，並依循「儒鴻企業環安衛及能
源政策」設置溫室氣體盤查推行委員會，由董事長室擔任主任委員，推動廠
區溫室氣體盤查，並以 2017 年作為公司溫室氣體盤查的基準年，推動溫室
氣體減量。此外，致力於用水回收，除了在製程蒸氣冷凝回收使用外，更引
進新技術改善廢水回收。 
公司簡介： 
儒鴻為台灣主要紡織業者，具染整、織布、成衣上下游一貫生產能力，主要
產品為各式成衣，並生產機能布料應用在各類運動休閒服飾上。國際各知名
服飾品牌及通路商如 NIKE、 UA、 LULULEMON 、 TARGET、 COSTCO 皆
為儒鴻的客戶。 
預估 4Q26 營收維持成長，稅後 EPS 6.09 元： 
儒鴻 3Q26 營收維持高檔為 105.72 億元，YoY+9.74%，QoQ+4.82%，毛利率
亦受惠客戶新產品出貨增加，產品組合優化及匯率相對友善等因素，順利提
升至 31.18%，稅後 EPS 為 6.36 元，符合預期。 
預期 4Q26 由於成衣價格上漲且客戶新品訂單持續出貨，可推動儒鴻營收維
持成長，預估儒鴻 4Q26 營收為 105.93 億元，YoY+14.34%， QoQ+0.20%，
毛利率 31.23%，稅後 EPS 6.09 元。 
美國期中選舉結束，市場不確定因素消除，儒鴻訂單能見度維持六個月左
右： 
美國期中選舉在民主黨獲勝的結果下，可預期極端的關稅政策不會再發生，
不確定性因素逐漸消除，不過目前也沒看到品牌端有追加訂單的動作出現，
預期未來幾個月的訂單及終端銷售會處於正常的水準。目前儒鴻的訂單能
見度維持在六個月左右，市場庫存也在相對健康水準，整體市況穩定。 
儒鴻持續投入自動化設備更新提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，
印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產： 
儒鴻持續擴增新產能及自動化設備更新以因應客戶需求，除了在台灣、越南
二地的生產設備持續更新外，印尼廠新產能也計劃在 4Q27 產能投產。目前
印尼廠的產能利用率維持滿載水準，印尼廠的生產需求未來的成長空間仍
大，預期等印尼廠三期投產後，可為儒鴻帶來下一段成長的動能。 
投資建議： 
預估儒鴻 2026 年營收為 408.71 億元，YoY+7.59%，稅後 EPS 24.79 元。2027
年預期在產能稼動維持高檔及客戶訂單穩定下，營收成長至 424.43 億元，
YoY+3.84%，稅後 EPS 25.52 元。考量：(1)3Q26 毛利率如預期的順利回升，
預期未來幾個季度的毛利率可維持現有水準。(2)持續投入自動化設備更新
提高生產效率，並擴建新產能因應客戶需求，印尼新廠預計 4Q27 投產，推
動長線的成長動能。綜上所述，對儒鴻的投資建議為 Trading Buy，目標價
385 元 (2027 年 PER 15.5X)。

個股報告

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。 
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

巧新(1563 TT) Trading Buy

2026、 2027 年營運動能向上，投資建議 Trading Buy。  
投資建議 Trading Buy ：巧新(1563 TT) 努力進行轉型，半導體
新事業於 2H26 逐漸取得進展，且再生鋁出貨占比也明顯拉升，
帶動毛利率明顯改善。2026 年獲利表現回升且 2027 年獲利能
力續揚。惟考量近期汽車零組件族群評價不易拉升，投資建議
Trading Buy，目標價 76 元 (以 2027 年 EPS 及 PE 15 倍評價)。  
3Q26 本業獲利小幅增長，因業外收入挹注，整體獲利顯著季增；
預估 4Q26 獲利相對下降：3Q26 營收 20.16 億元，QoQ+1.83%、
YoY+18.48%。受惠於再生鋁採用比重提升且匯率相對有利，毛
利率由 2Q26 的 24.32%提升至 25.90%。但因運輸成本上漲，營
業費用同步增長，營業利益 QoQ+6.72%、 YoY+211.71%。因業
外認列大筆匯兌利益，稅率表現穩定。稅後純益 2.47 億元，
QoQ+247.56%、 YoY+478.06%， EPS 1.46 元。4Q26 方面，預估
營收 19.21 億元，QoQ-4.70%、 YoY+16.97%。獲利能力方面，
受惠於再生鋁採用率高，預估毛利率提升至 27.54%。因運費相
對下降，預估營業利益 QoQ+15.87%、 YoY+342.49%。因 3Q26
已認列大筆匯兌收益，若以保守角度看待 4Q26 業外貢獻，預估
稅後純益 1.67 億元，QoQ-32.69%、YoY 轉虧為盈，EPS 0.98 元。 
預估 2026 年獲利表現回升，2027 年獲利能力續揚：2026 年方
面，輪圈出貨小幅提升，而半導體相關零組件自 2H26 開始貢獻
初步營收。預估 2026 年營收 77.89 億元，YoY+11.63%。隨著再
生鋁採用率提升且營收規模增長，毛利率有望逐季提升，預估
2026 年毛利率提升至 24.97%，營業利益 YoY+43.13%。若業外
較無匯兌損失影響，預估稅後純益 6.16 億元，YoY+93.56%，
EPS 3.64 元。2027 年方面，根據車廠預示量，鋁圈出貨量將由
2026 年的 60 萬顆提升至 70 萬顆，有助於帶動車用產品營收增
長。而半導體營收占比有機會提升至 5~10%，將提供額外營收
動能。因再生鋁採用比重提升，且半導體產品毛利率相對較高，
2027 年毛利率及獲利能力有望優於 2026 年。預估 2027 年營收
92.77 億元，YoY+19.10%，稅後純益 8.56 億元，YoY+38.99%，
EPS 5.06 元。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

株式会社大和総研 丸の内オフィス 〒100-6756 東京都千代田区丸の内一丁目 9番 1号 グラントウキョウ ノースタワー

2026年 11月 16日 全7頁

Indicators Update 
2026年 7-9月期 GDP（1次速報） 
個人消費の弱さと外需の下押しで、4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に

経済調査部 チーフエコノミスト 神田 慶司 
  エコノミスト 畑中 宏仁

[要約] 
◼ 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率▲0.1％（前期比▲0.0％）と小幅なが
らも 4 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。設備投資が増加した一方、個人消費が 2
四半期連続で減少し、輸出減と輸入増から外需も成長率を押し下げた。在庫変動を除
く実質最終需要は前期比年率▲2.1％と低迷するなど、内容は悪い。 
◼ 10-12月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率＋0％台前半と小幅ながらもプラス成長に転
じると見込んでいる。個人消費は小幅に増加し、設備投資も増加するだろう。輸出は
米国向けを中心に増加するものの、輸入の伸びが上回り、外需の寄与度は 2四半期連
続のマイナスになるとみている。リスク要因は AI関連需要の下振れと中東情勢の悪化
であり、輸出や個人消費などへの影響に注意が必要だ。

※当社は、11月 20日（金）に「第231回日本経済予測」の発表を予定している。

実質GDPは小幅ながらも 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長 
実質GDP成長率は前期比年率▲0.1％と市場予想を下回り、内容も悪い 
2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDP成長率は、1次速報値で前期比年率▲0.1％（前期比▲0.0％）だ
った。小幅ながらも 4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となり、市場予想（QUICK調査中央値、前期
比年率＋0.2％）を下回った。なお、12月 8日に公表される 2次速報では 2025年度国民経済計
算年次推計の反映により、幅広い需要項目が改定される可能性がある。 
設備投資が増加に転じ、実質雇用者報酬の増加が続くなど緩やかな景気の拡大基調は続いて
いるとみられるが、在庫変動を除いた実質最終需要は前期比年率▲2.1％であり、個人消費が 2
四半期連続で減少するなど内容は悪い。 
また、交易利得・損失は7-9月期で▲11.4兆円（年率換算額）で1、実質GDPに交易利得・損
失を加えた実質 GDI（国内総所得）成長率は前期比年率▲1.6％だった。原油高などによる交易
条件の悪化で所得が海外に流出し、交易損失は前期から 2.2兆円拡大した。国内の所得環境は
GDPで見る以上に悪化したが、政府のエネルギー高対策が家計や企業への影響を一定程度緩和
した。 
実質 GDPを需要項目別に見ると（図表 2）、民需関連では個人消費・住宅投資が減少した一方、
設備投資は増加した。公需関連では政府消費と公共投資が減少した。公的在庫変動については、
7-9月期に大規模な石油の国家備蓄放出が行われなかったため、実質 GDP成長率への寄与度は
前期比＋0.5％pt と成長率を大きく押し上げた。外需関連では輸出減と輸入増により、純輸出
は実質GDP成長率を押し下げた。 
図表2：実質 GDP成長率と需要項目別の寄与度

（出所）内閣府統計より大和総研作成

1 交易条件（＝輸出デフレーター/輸入デフレーター）の変化による実質所得の増減で、プラスの場合は「交易 
利得」、マイナスの場合は「交易損失」。公表値は 2020暦年連鎖価格のデフレーターから算出されているた 
め（基準年の交易利得・損失額はゼロ）、交易利得・損失額はあくまでも基準年からの交易条件の変化の影響 
である点に留意が必要。

### Page 3

1

3 / 7

GDPデフレーターは前年同期比＋2.4％と 19四半期連続のプラスだった。また、単位労働コ
スト（＝名目雇用者報酬÷実質 GDP）は同＋4.4％と 14四半期連続のプラスで、賃金面からの
物価上昇圧力は足元でも継続している（巻末の関連指標①下段左）。

個人消費は、4-6月期が下方修正された上で 2四半期連続の減少 
個人消費は前期比▲0.3％と 2四半期連続で減少した。公表前の 4-6月期の個人消費は同＋
0.0％だったが、インバウンド消費額が上方修正されたことで同▲0.3％へと引き下げられた2。
7-9月期は一部耐久財の反動減や、9 月の天候不順といった一時的な下押し要因があったもの
の、所得環境が改善する中で 4-6月期から 2四半期連続で減少したところに、個人消費の弱さ
が見られる。 
国内家計最終消費支出を財・サービス別に見ると、耐久財（前期比▲3.5％）や半耐久財
（同▲1.4％）、非耐久財（同▲0.2％）がいずれも減少した一方、サービス（同＋0.1％）は増
加した（巻末の関連指標①上段左）。内閣府によると、耐久財ではエアコンなどが、半耐久財
では衣服などが、非耐久財ではその他個人ケア用器具（歯ブラシなど）などが押し下げたとい
う。4-6月期には、自動車税の環境性能割廃止や、2027年 4月に予定されているエアコンの省
エネ基準引き上げを見越した買い替え需要が耐久財消費を後押ししたが、その効果がはく落し
た。衣料品などの不振は天候不順が影響したとみられる。一方、サービスでは教育や携帯電話
通信料などが押し上げたという。 
実質雇用者報酬（家計最終消費支出デフレーターで実質化）は前期比＋0.3％と6四半期連続
で増加した（巻末の関連指標①上段右）。労働力調査（総務省）に見る雇用者数は同 0.0％で、
雇用者数で除した1人あたり実質雇用者報酬は同＋0.3％と6四半期連続で増加した（前年同期
比では3四半期連続のプラス）。

設備投資は3四半期ぶりの増加、住宅投資は2四半期連続の減少 
設備投資は前期比＋1.6％と 3四半期ぶりに増加した。建設投資は減少したものの、4-6月期
に特殊要因で減少していた研究開発投資で反動増が表れたほか、機械投資が増加したようだ。
内閣府によると、機械投資はサーバーや半導体製造装置などが増加に寄与したという。 
住宅投資は前期比▲1.1％と2四半期連続で減少した。原材料費高騰による建設資材価格の上
昇や人件費の上昇が重しとなっており、住宅着工戸数は 6月にかけて増加していたものの、7
月以降は下落に転じている（巻末の関連指標②上段右）。

2 家計最終消費支出は国内消費支出から非居住者家計の国内での直接購入（インバウンド消費）を控除して算
出されているため、インバウンド消費の上方修正は家計最終消費支出の下方修正につながる。

### Page 4

1

4 / 7

公共投資は 2四半期連続の減少、政府消費は6四半期ぶりに減少 
公共投資は前期比▲1.1％と2四半期連続で減少した。資材価格の高騰や人手不足などが重し
になっている上、2025年度補正予算に盛り込まれた公共事業の執行が一巡したとみられる。 
政府消費は前期比▲0.0％と小幅ながらも6四半期ぶりに減少した。内閣府によると、医療・
介護費が増加したものの、中間消費を中心にデフレーターが上昇したことが実質成長率の押し
下げにつながったという。 
また、実質 GDP成長率に対する公的在庫変動の寄与度は、7-9月期は大規模な石油の国家備
蓄放出が行われなかったため前期比＋0.5％ptとなり、成長率を大幅に押し上げた。後述のよ
うに原油の代替調達の進展によって、輸入の増加が実質 GDP成長率を押し下げたが、公的在庫
変動の増加がおおむね相殺した形だ。

輸出減と輸入増により、外需寄与度は前期比▲0.6％pt 
輸出は前期比▲0.2％と4四半期ぶりに減少した。財は増加したが、サービスが減少した。内
閣府によると、財では半導体製造装置や石油製品、基礎化学製品などが増加したという。サー
ビスでは、4-6月期に特殊要因で大幅に増加していた研究開発サービスに加え、インバウンド
や産業財産権等使用料なども減少したようだ。 
輸入は前期比＋2.5％と 2 四半期ぶりに増加した。サービスは減少したものの、財が増加し
た。財では、中東情勢の影響により 4-6月期に減少していた石油・天然ガスが代替調達の進展
によって増加したほか、非鉄金属やサーバー等も増加したようだ。サービスでは、アウトバウ
ンドは増加したものの、内閣府によると著作権等使用料や研究開発サービス、専門・経営コン
サルティングサービスなどが減少したという。 
輸出の減少と輸入の増加により、純輸出（外需）の実質 GDP成長率に対する寄与度は前期比
▲0.6％ptと、4四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。

10-12月期の実質 GDPは内需と輸出に支えられ、小幅のプラス成長か 
2026年 10-12月期の実質 GDP成長率は前期比年率＋0％台前半と、小幅ながらもプラス成長
に転じると見込んでいる。 
個人消費は小幅な増加に転じるとみている。現金給与総額は前年同月比で 3％を超える伸び
が続いており、10月以降は各地で最低賃金が引き上げられていることから名目賃金の高い伸び
は続くだろう。一方、食料品等を中心に物価上昇率は高まっている。エネルギー価格について
も、資源価格高騰がラグを持って反映されることに加え、補助の一時終了や縮小も物価の押し
上げ要因となり、10-12月期の実質賃金上昇率を押し下げるだろう。設備投資は建設資材価格
と人件費の上昇が建設投資を下押しするものの、省力化投資やAI関連投資を背景に増加基調が

### Page 5

1

5 / 7

続くと見込む。 
輸出は増加に転じるとみている。米国の設備投資はAI関連需要を背景に増加が続くだろう。
米国内での設備投資増加は米国向け輸出の追い風となり、財輸出は増加すると見込んでいる。
一方、インバウンドは中国政府による日本への渡航自粛要請が続く中、増加は緩やかなものと
なるだろう。 
輸入は増加するとみている。原油輸入の回復が続くことに加え、AI関連需要を背景に電算機
類等の輸入も増加するだろう。輸入の増加が輸出の増加を上回ることで、外需の寄与度は 2四
半期連続のマイナスになると見込んでいる。 
10-12月期以降を含めたリスク要因は、AI関連需要の下振れと中東情勢の悪化である。足元
の米国経済では設備投資が景気を下支えしており、とりわけデータセンターや半導体などの IT
関連投資がけん引役となっている。AI関連サービスの世界的な利用拡大に伴い、計算能力の増
強に向けた大型投資が今後も続く可能性は高い。一方で、一部のAI関連企業では収益性や財務
面への懸念が広がっているほか、米中間選挙でAIデータセンター建設の一時停止や規制強化な
どを訴えた民主党急進左派候補者が当選するなど、AI投資を取り巻く環境には変化の兆しも見
られる。ハイパースケーラーが設備投資の拡大ペースを落とせば、半導体や情報通信機器など
の需要を下押しし、関連産業の生産減や輸出減などを通じて実体経済にも波及するだろう3。ま
た、中東情勢が悪化し、原油価格がさらに高騰すれば、物価高を通じて個人消費を一段と下押
しするだろう。 
2027年度までの経済見通しの詳細などについては、11月 20日に発表予定の「第 231回日本
経済予測」を参照されたい。

3 詳細は、畑中宏仁・神田慶司「ハイパースケーラーの投資動向が世界経済に与える影響」（大和総研レポー
ト、2026年11月 12日）を参照。

• Anticipating another strong year into 2027 — MPI has maintained its 
current guidance for capacity plan into next year: year end-2026 capacity 
targets of VPC 2m and MEMS 3.5m, and 2027 MEMS expansion is expected 
to be at least another 2.5m. However, we see potential upside thanks to 
robust customer demand and market share gain. CPO insertion 2 is under 
final evaluation and insertion 3 already starts engineering  shipments  to 
more than 5 international customers across foundry, OSAT and fabless 
segments. The company sees strong growth momentum of probe card into 
next year and CPO equipment contribution at single digit in 2027 with more 
upside into 2028.
• We stay constructive on MPI despite recent market concerns regarding AI 
demand slowing down. The key drivers are continuing to ramp-up at 
existing AI ASIC customers, incremental contributions from new AI/non-AI 
ASIC and CPU customers, further MEMS capacity expansion, and potential 
upside from CPO and microbump probe-card technology.

### Page 2

MPI (6223.TWO)
11 November 2026 Citi Research
2
MPI
Valuation
Our target price for MPI is set at NT$7,200, based on a target PE multiple of 52x applied to our 2027-28E EPS average. We
believe our PE target, at the high end of MPI's 3-year forward PE range, is justified given MPI’s earnings power remains
structurally higher than that in the previous cycles, broadening demand across AI ASIC, additional CPU opportunities and non-
AI ASIC wins. We expect MPI to see accelerating earnings growth ahead supported by sustainable sales growth and an

improving mix. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027E/28E P/B of 23x/15x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the MPI stock as High Risk for its share-price volatility. Key downside risks that could
prevent MPI shares from reaching our target price include 1) slowdown in AI ASIC demand; 2) slower-than-expected adoption
of MEMS probe cards; 3) intensifying competition from global and regional peers; 4) slow development of the CPO equipment

business.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

## Document 165: 261112_daiwa_HH

Source PDF: all report pdf/261112_daiwa_HH.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review Hon Hai’s 3Q26 results, announced 12 November. 
We are impressed with its opex control, which led to solid operating margin 
expansion despite gross margin dilution from rising AI server contribution.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat. Net income was TWD78bn (EPS: 
TWD5.53), 25% higher than our estimate (TWD62bn) and 10% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus (TWD70bn). Revenue was TWD3.0tn (+19% 
QoQ; +46% YoY) with better-than-expected smartphone contribution. The 
gross margin came in at 6.02% (vs. 6.12% in 2Q26), a 0.1pp decline QoQ 
on a rising cloud segment mix. That said, the operating margin expanded 
0.07pp to 3.83%, driven by strong opex control and better leverage.

Key updates: Hon Hai reaffirms its positive view of its 2027 revenue 
momentum, targeting double-digit growth YoY (similar to our forecast). The 
company believes its cloud business will remain a key growth driver (54% 
of total revenue in 3Q26). Regarding total AI server racks, Hon Hai expects 
to see high-double-digit growth in 2027 (vs. our expectation of c.30% YoY 
growth from GPU server plus a rising ASIC contribution; however, as ASIC 
AI servers typically utilise consignment models, its AI server revenue 
growth will be slightly diluted). As for smartphones, considering Hon Hai’s 
key clients’ models command high ASPs, we believe the impact from rising 
memory costs will be relatively smaller, but we expect the PC segment to 
decline YoY due to the negative impact from rising component costs.

During the 3Q26 results call, AI server end demand from the US was the 
hottest question, especially after the US mid-term elections. We believe 
management’s view on hyperscalers’ and neocloud’s near-term demand 
remains positive, but the long-term impact must be closely monitored. Also, 
as investors are worried about the AI server capex slowdown from CSPs, 
management also indicated that there would be new end demand from 
sovereign and enterprise groups, which could act as a second growth 
wave. For 4Q26, the company is guiding for >15% revenue growth QoQ 
and YoY given the ICT peak season along with rising AI server revenue (up 
by 5-15% QoQ due to ongoing AI server shipment strength).

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 10-12% to reflect 
higher ASP assumptions for AI servers. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating but 
trim the 12M TP to TWD320, using a lower target PER of 13x (prior 16x; vs. 
its past-3-year range of 8-18x) on our 1-year forward EPS, given the overall 
weaker sentiment. Downside risk: low demand for AI servers.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E revenue are above the consensus by 2-3%, 
likely as we are more optimistic on Hon Hai’s AI server shipments.

12 November 2026

3Q26 review: solid opex control

➢ OPM expansion on strong scale leverage despite GM contraction  
➢ Demand outlook remains promising even after US mid-term elections 
➢ Reiterating Buy (1) rating; trimming 12M TP to TWD320

Company profile 
Established in 1974, Hon Hai Precision Industry (Hon Hai) started out making plastic products, 
connector products and cable assemblies. It is now the largest electronics manufacturing service (EMS) 
provider globally in terms of design, manufacturing, global logistics and after-market service.

世芯-KY(3661 TT) Buy

新客戶加入，挹注 2027 年營運成長，建議 Buy。  
投資建議：世芯-KY 受惠北美 CSP 客戶 N3 AI 加速器量產，
2026 年營運大幅成長；展望 2027 年，除既有 N3 專案持續放量
外，美系新客戶約 10 億美元專案預計自 2Q27 起貢獻量產營收，
將推動營收及獲利進一步成長。中長期 N2 AI 加速器亦持續推
進，預計 2028 年進入量產，成為下一階段成長動能，預估 2026
年及 2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為 113.66 元及 193.02 元。惟隨量產
(COT)業務占比提高，營收成長未必同步帶動毛利率提升，且
CSP 持續提升自主設計能力，將是影響世芯-KY 長期商業模式
及評價的重要風險。我們認為上述風險主要影響中長期估值上
限，但未來 12 個月仍受惠 N3 放量及新客戶加入，基本面成長
趨勢明確。因此建議 Buy，目標價 4,635 元 (PER24X)。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元：北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片已
正式啟動量產出貨，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億
元，QoQ+240.99%。晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營
收產生約 1 億美元之負面影響。受量產業務營收佔比拉升影響，
3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個百
分點。3Q26 稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
取得美系新客戶訂單: 世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽
訂重大合約並展開執行，該案量約 10 億美元，合約已正式生效
並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季增加，該專案確
定應用於 AI 領域。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對
象，非中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出
規模，研判最具可能性之潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之
北美雲端巨頭；世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切
入合作，有效緩解客戶端工程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指
出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全新商業模式之開端，
該新客戶將躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。

2 群益投顧 
  世芯-KY ESG：  
世芯電子身為全球 IC 設計服務領導廠商，為確保所有產品皆為符合環境安
全衛生規定之綠色產品，對供應鏈管理不遺餘力，與供應商合作將綠色環保
的政策及概念推廣到整個供應，並一起朝向減少地球環境之衝擊共同努力，
其中主要包括： 
有害物質管制 
符合世芯電子相關規範及國內、外法令等國際環境規範 
滿足世芯電子新外包商評鑑程序 
滿足世芯電子外包商管理程序 
滿足世芯電子環境管理物質管理程序 
歐盟危害物質限用指令 (EU RoHS Compliance) 
歐盟新化學品政策 (REACH) 
無鹵材料 (Halogen Free) 
綠色物料之承認管理 
供應商提出第三方對產品生產材料所作的環境物質分析報告或證明文件。 
綠色資訊平台之建置 
建構系統化之 IT 資料庫輔助管理系統，藉此平台，便利上下游廠商綠色零
件之交換，並帶動上游廠商綠色零件供應的能力，降低環保要求的衝擊程
度。 
建立環境管理系統 - 供應商必須擁有 ISO14001 環境管理系統或其他相關
環境管理系統相關驗證。 
無衝突礦產保證書-(Declaration of Metal Conflict-Free) 
衝突金屬係指來自剛果民主共和國衝突礦區之礦物，類別有鈮鉭鐵礦，錫
石，黑鎢礦與黃金等。這些礦物提煉成鉭(Ta)、錫(Sn)、鎢(W) 、鈷(Co) 和
金 (Au) 等，分別用於電子和其他產品。世芯電子強烈要求供應商提供的原
物料中不得間接或直接使用衝突礦產。 
企業社會責任 (CSR) - 確保供應商落實企業社會責任。 
業務連續性計劃 (BCP) 
供應商須有一套基於業務運行規律的流程，使組織在突發事件面前能夠迅
速作出反應，以確保關鍵業務功能可以持續，而不造成業務中斷或業務流程
本質的改變。 
綠色管理稽核 - 世芯-KY 每年持續對供應商進行稽核、改善及教育訓練，
經由系統化運作與管理，確保提供品質精良之卓越產品給客戶與消費者。

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

3 群益投顧 
N3 貢獻增加，3Q26 營收 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%，稅後淨利 32.51 億
元，QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元： 
北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器晶片 已於 05/2026 正式啟動量產出貨，之後營
收逐月向上，世芯-KY 3Q26 營收大幅上揚至 260.51 億元，QoQ+240.99%。
在產能限制方面，晶圓、載板與先進封裝產能吃緊對 3Q26 營收產生約 1 億
美元之負面影響，若無此類供應限制，單季營收原可增加 1 億美元。資料中
心以外之其他應用在 3Q26 合計創造超過 1 億美元營收，其中中國車用 ADAS
需求維持強勁，客戶次世代晶片如期於 4Q26 進行流片(Tape-out)。  
3Q26 各應用佔公司營收比重，HPC 佔公司營收比重 87%，佔比仍為最高，
相較 2Q26 的 50%與 1Q26 的 70%大幅提升。消費性電子(Consumer)佔比降
至 8%，低於 2Q26 的 16%與 1Q26 的 17%。 Networking 佔 5%，較 2Q26 的
32%與 1Q26 的 10%下降。利基型(Niche，含超級電腦、虛擬貨幣、柏青哥
機台、醫療設備與監視系統)佔 0%，低於 2Q26 的 1%與 1Q26 的 3%。  
製程營收比重，N3/N2 佔公司營收比重大幅躍升至 80%，較 2Q26 的 47%與
1Q26 的 21%大幅增加，主因北美 CSP 客戶之 N3 加速器持續放量出貨。
7nm/5nm 佔比降至 15%，較 2Q26 的 40%下滑。16/12nm 佔比為 3%，較 2Q26
的 6%下降。28nm 及以下製程佔比為 1%，低於 2Q26 的 7%。  
地區營收分布隨著 N3 加速器放量出貨，地區分布再度出現結構性轉變：北
美地區佔比穩居第一大市場，達 84%(2Q26 為 51%， 1Q26 為 23%)。亞太地
區佔比降至 12%(2Q26 為 25%， 1Q26 為 47%)，車用晶片出貨維持穩健。其
他地區佔 4%(2Q26 為 17%， 1Q26 為 22%)。日本地區佔 1%(2Q26 為 7%，
1Q26 為 8%)。   
毛利率方面，3Q26 毛利率下滑至 18.04%，較 2Q26 的 34.84%下滑 16.80 個
百分點，為產品組合的最差情境，主因量產營收比重大幅拉升，致使委託設
計 (NRE)營收貢獻比例低於預期，加上數個專案之 NRE 認列產生遞延所致。
不過營業利益與稅後淨利同步創高。3Q26 世芯-KY 稅後淨利 32.51 億元，
QoQ+98.41%，稅後 EPS 37.67 元。 
N2 專案將在 2026 年底 Tape-Out：  
世芯-KY 承接之美系 CSP 次世代 N2 AI 加速器專案，將採用異質小晶片
(Heterogeneous Chiplet) 架構。晶片總面積維持在約 4 個光罩尺寸，但相較
於 N3 世代，N2 專案之運算晶粒(Compute Die) 配置、內部互連架構
(Interconnect)及封裝型式(2.5D/3D)均出現顯著改動。由於小晶片設計涉及
多個獨立晶( 包括主運算晶粒與 I/O 晶粒) ，各晶粒之開發週轉時間
(Turnaround Time)不同，專案將採取分階段進行多次定案(Tape-out)之模式。
2026 年底 Tape-out ， 2027 年底放量 
次世代 N2 加速器已進入最終實作階段，預計自 4Q26 開始展開流片，目標
於 4Q27 達成小量試產(Low volume production) ，並於 2028 年迎來量產放
量，推動世芯-KY 自 2028 年初邁入全新成長階段。N2 與 N3 在晶片設計複
雜度上有顯著差異，N3 主要為 SoC 設計結合 2.5D 封裝，而 N2 雖維持 2.5D
封裝架構，但已全面轉向 Chiplet 架構搭配全環繞閘極(GAA)製程技術。世
芯 -KY 負責 N2 晶片各個部分之實體設計並進行整合，其在 N2 專案之設計
參與深度與整體專案規模均顯著大於 N3。專案不依賴特定同業之第三方 IP，

### Page 4

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

4 群益投顧 
均採用通用型解決方案。在商業與生產模式上，世芯-KY 為 N2 之唯一實體
設計服務商，而在量產階段，客戶目前承諾僅會有客戶本身與世芯-KY 兩方
直接向台積電下單投片，世芯-KY 在 N2 之供應鏈地位與競爭優勢將較 N3
更為穩固。關於小晶片整合，包含 I/O Die 在內之晶片均由世芯-KY 統籌實
體設計，未來若因應 UALink 或 NVLink Fusion 等技術規格變更推出其他版
本之 I/O Die，世芯-KY 亦具備承接物理設計之先機。 
COT 合作模式短中期是有利世芯-KY，但長期要觀察 CSP 廠的全面自主設
計的進度： 
各大 CSP 為降低對傳統純 ASIC 廠商(如 Broadcom)高定價的依賴並實現
Cost-down，正全面轉向 COT 模式(客戶負責前端設計，世芯負責實體設計
與後端整合)。專案朝向「運算晶粒(Compute die)與  I/O 晶粒分工設計」發
展，世芯已與潛在客戶洽談負責運算晶粒並與外部 I/O 晶片整合的模式。
CSP 客戶轉向 COT 模式，本質上源於 3 項戰略考量： 
避免供應商鎖定(Vendor Lock-in) ：多數競爭對手採用垂直整合的封閉模式
(Captive Model)，從 IP、設計服務到封裝一條龍自主掌控，客戶一旦導入即
難以切換。CSP 客戶對此高度警惕，傾向採用第三方開放解決方案以保有供
應商多元化的彈性，降低單一供應商造成的議價劣勢與供應鏈風險。 
成本透明度與預算自主性：COT 模式下，客戶對晶圓、IP、設計服務及其他
供應鏈成本具有較高的掌握度，實際報酬及成本分攤方式則依個別專案合
約而定。相較於封閉模式下由供應商主導整體產品定價，COT 模式有助於
客戶提高成本控管能力與預算可預測性。 
規模擴張下的成本節省潛力： AI 晶片專案的出貨量與單位價值均遠高於傳
統晶片，隨著量產規模持續放大，COT 模式下透明且固定的利潤結構所帶
來的成本節省效益將等比例放大，對 CSP 客戶的財務影響不容忽視。 
然而，COT 模式對世芯-KY 的財務表現構成若干限制： 
COT 模式下的獲利空間取決於合約條件與服務範圍：COT 模式下，客戶通
常對專案成本及供應鏈有較高的掌握度，世芯-KY 的獲利則取決於其承接
的設計服務範圍、專案管理責任及個別合約的報酬安排。相較於自行掌握完
整產品定價與供應鏈的模式，若專案採成本加成或事先約定服務報酬的方
式，世芯-KY 透過晶片價格上漲或供應鏈成本變動擴大利潤的空間可能較
為有限。因此，未來毛利率及獲利表現，仍須觀察各專案的服務內容、合約
條件及營收組合變化。 
多方協作帶來的執行複雜度與供應鏈風險： COT 模式涉及晶圓廠、IP 供應
商、基板廠、封裝廠及測試廠等多個外部夥伴的協同整合，任一環節的產能
短缺或交期延誤均可能波及整體專案進度。世芯-KY 在 N7 世代曾因 ABF 基
板短缺而承受供應鏈壓力，此一歷史案例揭示開放生態系模式下供應鏈管
理的內在脆弱性。儘管公司已大幅增加供應商來源以分散風險，但多源管理
本身亦帶來額外的協調成本與品質控管挑戰。 
IP 自主能力缺失，技術話語權受限：世芯-KY 目前選擇與 Synopsys 等第三
方 IP 供應商合作，而非自行開發 IP。此策略雖降低研發資本支出，卻也意
味著公司在核心技術環節缺乏自主掌控能力，在特定 IP 出現供應限制或授
權條件變動時，可能面臨被動局面，長期而言亦限制公司在技術差異化上的
發展空間。

### Page 5

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

5 群益投顧 
規模成長不必然帶動利潤率同步提升：在固定利潤百分比的框架下，營收規
模的擴大主要反映的是晶圓與供應鏈成本的等比例放大，而非公司自身附
加價值的提升。換言之，世芯-KY 的獲利成長高度依賴出貨量的持續擴張，
而非利潤率的結構性改善，此一特性使公司的盈利模式更接近於量能驅動
型 (Volume-driven)，而非價值驅動型(Value-driven)。  
整體而言，世芯-KY 等 IC 設計服務公司的商業模式恰好能針對不同客戶的
能力缺口提供精準介入。無論客戶需求是後端實體設計落地、部分實體設計
協作、I/O Chiplet 的 IP 整合，乃至純量產委外，世芯均具備對應的服務能
量。此種高度彈性的模組化接案模式，使公司得以滲透不同成熟度的客戶群
體，有效降低拿單門檻。 
總而言之，CSP 晶片自研能力的持續演進是評估世芯-KY 長期投資價值不
可迴避的核心風險。從演進路徑來看，CSP 通常由依賴完整 ASIC 服務起步，
逐步過渡至主導架構設計但委外實體落地，最終邁向全面設計自主。世芯-
KY 目前所享有的設計服務需求，本質上是 CSP 處於此過渡階段所產生的結
構性紅利，一旦 CSP 設計能力全面成熟，世芯-KY 的角色將面臨從「策略
性設計夥伴」退化為「可替代供應鏈中介」的根本性風險，商業模式的可持
續性將受到挑戰。短期而言，N3 乃至 N2 先進製程實體設計的高度複雜性，
以及多代合作累積的工程默契，仍構成一定的切換成本緩衝。然而隨著 EDA
工具智能化持續壓低設計門檻，此一護城河的侵蝕速度是要警惕。群益認
為，世芯-KY 能否在當前紅利窗口期內成功深化 IP 能力、拓展先進封裝技
術佈局並實現客戶多元化，將是決定其長期商業模式能否升級躍遷的關鍵
觀察指標。 
N3 專案為 2026 年和 2027 年主要的營收貢獻： 
世芯-KY 目前有超過 60 項 FinFET 專案和 20 個以上 CoWoS 案子，平均每
年 20~30 new tape-out，且獲得多個 N3 專案，也有 N2 專案進行中。其中最
重要的專案是為北美 CSP 開發的 N3 AI 加速器(Trainium 3)。該專案在 4Q25
啟動，2Q26 末已進入量產。N5 完整的專案營收貢獻約 35 億美元，N3 的營
收貢獻會高很多，除主要客戶專案之外，世芯-KY 還贏得來自北美客戶的多
個 N3 專案，包括網路客戶以及北美和歐洲的主要網路設備廠商。 
現行 N3 晶片已符合各項測試標準且良率維持在合理水準，目前正全速出
貨，為 2026 年最大營收貢獻專案，亦將維持為 2027 年最大營收來源。管理
層預期當前世代專案將量產約 2 年時間，隨後由次世代接替。該專案預期於
4Q26 營收呈現小幅季增，並於 2027 年呈現大幅增長。世芯-KY 過去因營運
資金有限而在該專案初期未深化溝通，但後續成功自競爭對手處贏回此專
案，目前主要營運與產能限制已獲得解決，已做好承接更大出貨規模之準
備。 
取得美系新客戶訂單，預期於 2027 年躍升為世芯-KY 第二大客戶： 
世芯-KY 確認已與知名美系大型巨頭簽訂重大合約並展開執行，該專案整
體生命週期累計營收規模約 10 億美元，量產營收預計自 2Q27 開始貢獻，
後續逐季增加。已正式生效並啟動，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季
維持季增趨勢，該專案確定應用於 AI 領域，推估採用台積電 N3 先進製程，
專案資產由雙方共同擁有。此新客戶為世芯-KY 此前未曾合作過之對象，非
中系且非新創企業，具備市場領導地位與龐大資本支出規模，市場推測可能

### Page 6

個股報告 
                                        2026 年 11 月 13 日

6 群益投顧 
為潛在對象為積極推進自研 AI 加速器之北美雲端巨頭 Meta 或 Microsoft；
世芯-KY 憑藉深厚後端物理設計與工程實力切入合作，有效緩解客戶端工
程資源吃緊之痛點，管理層明確指出此案絕非一次性業務，而是世芯-KY 全
新商業模式之開端，具備良好獲利能力且長期可持續，該新客戶自 2Q27 起
之量產放量將推動其躍升為世芯-KY 2027 年第二大客戶。 
2027 年 N3 案子貢獻持續，及美系新客戶加入，預估 2026 年和 2027 年稅後
EPS 分別為 113.66 元和 193.02 元： 
世芯-KY 2026 年 ~2028 年營收將會再大幅成長，因為 1)北美大客戶新世代
產品 N3 量產和下一代設計合作，公司預計其關鍵客戶在 2028 年前將有強
勁且可持續的營收貢獻；營收從 2Q26 開始顯著躍升。2)美系大型巨頭新客
戶的 10 億美元，量產(MP)營收貢獻預計自 2Q27 後逐季季增。3)車用業務
ADAS 方面，預計產品將成為從 2026 年開始的主要營收貢獻者之一。下一
代產品的開發已在進行中，確保營收連續性並為業務帶來強勁動能，直到
2028 年。 
受惠於 N3 AI加速器量產出貨小幅季增，疊加次世代 N2 專案展開流片(Tape-
out)與大型設計案啟動，4Q26 營收預估將達到 8.5~9 億美元區間，改寫單季
歷史新高。由於量產營收比重仍高達 80%以上，4Q26 毛利率預估將維持在
18~19.5%區間，公司預期 2026 年全年毛利率將達 20%以上。預估 2026 年
稅後 EPS 113.66 元。 
邁入 2027 年，1Q27 營收預估小幅拉回，自 2Q27 起，隨著規模達 10 億美
元的美國新大客戶量產營收正式啟動，營運將重啟逐季強勁增長動能，單季
營收將突破 10 億美元大關。3Q27 至 4Q27 在現有 N3 專案擴大出貨以及次
世代 N2 加速器於 4Q27 展開小量產(Low volume production) 挹注下，預估
2027 年稅後 EPS 193.02 元。 
2028 年次世代 N2 加速器進入量產： 
展望 2028 年，世芯-KY 大客戶的次世代 N2 加速器進入大規模量產階段，
由於 N2 專案轉向全環繞閘極(GAA)與 Chiplet 先進架構，世芯-KY 表示將
負責全晶片實體設計與整合，晶片價值量與專案規模將顯著超越 N3 世代。
因此管理層評估 2028 年營收具備再次翻倍之潛力，但群益先以保守情境預
估。 
在營運擴張過程中，世芯-KY 面臨兩大核心營運限制，分別為營運資金
(Working Capital)承載力以及高階研發人力瓶頸。在資金層面，管理層明確
指出 2028 年營收成長的瓶頸已非市場需求，而是資產負債表的承載能力。
由於雲端服務商(CSP)先進製程晶圓與先進封裝代工代採購金額極為龐大，
支撐 40 億美元以上營收需要巨額營運資金墊付晶圓廠貨款，公司資產負債
表結構將直接決定能承接的量產規模上限。在人力層面，單一先進製程大型
專案往往需要高達 150 位工程師投入，在 N3 出貨、N2 實作與新客戶專案
同步推進下，研發資源短缺成為主要限制，迫使公司採取高度篩選專案之策
略，僅承接具備實質商業營收之大型案件。為突破研發人力天花板，世芯-
KY 目前正積極對外擴編，預計 2026 年底員工人數將突破千人規模，並在
前端實作與驗證導入專屬內部 AI 訓練模型以大幅節省中段研發人力，但在
最終實體設計階段仍高度仰賴頂尖工程人力，高階人才擴充速度將是公司
長期承接新專案的關鍵決定因素。

建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감

방산 납품 계획에 따른 일시적 실적 급감 
지상방산 매출 1조 2,450억원으로 전년비 41%, 영업이익 217억원으로 전년비 
96.2% 급감하며 영업이익률은 1.7% 기록. 실적 급감에 대해 사측은 올해 주요 사업
의 납품 일정이 4분기에 집중됨에 따라 3분기 인도 물량이 적었던 것과 대전 사업장 
생산 중단에 따른 영향 때문이라고 설명. 단, 4분기에는 당초 계획에 따라 주요 사업의 
납품 물량이 집중되어 있어 실적이 개선될 것이고 대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 내에 정
상 가돌 하는 것이 목표라고 밝힘 
수주잔고 증가세 지속으로 성장 지속 가능할 것 
3분기 말 수주잔고 35조원으로 전년비 12.9% 증가. 이를 통한 지상방산 부문 성장 
지속 가능할 것으로 판단. 참고로 사측은 내년 매출 15% 수준 성장 전망 유지. 하지
만 금리 상승에 따른 글로벌 업종 평균 밸류에이션 하락에 따른 방산 부문 적용 멀티
플 하향 조정 그리고 상장 연결회사(한화오션, 한화시스템, 쎄트렉아이)와 상장 비연결
회사(한국항공우주)의 주가 하락 반영 영향으로 목표주가는 하향 조정 
8월 미 육군 차륜형 자주포 사업에서 시제 사업자로 선정. 향후 K9MH는 미 육군의 
작전 운용 개념에 맞춰 맞춤 조정을 거친 후에 특별한 결함 혹은 결격 사유가 없으면 
최종 계약 체결 예정. 동시에 미 육군의 궤도형 자주포 사업에도 참여할 계획. 뿐만 아
니라 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등으로 신시장 개척 역시 지속되고 있다는 점도 긍
정적 
 Analyst 이재광 
rheejaekwang@lssec.co.kr

## Document 274: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-04

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Earnings Review Company Analysis | 이차전지/화학 | 2026. 11. 4  
일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사 Capa 우려

AMPC 4,169억원 포함 영업이익 컨센서스 대폭 상회 
동사는 8일 매출액 9조 6,434억원(YoY 59%, QoQ 27%), AMPC 포함한 영업이익 
7,560억원(OPM 7.8%, YoY 26%, QoQ 567%)로 시장 Consensus(공정공시한 8일 
기준 3,264억원)을 대폭 상회하는 어닝 서프라이즈 발표. 회사는 ESS 출하 급증과 고
전압 Mid-Ni EV 파우치 확대, 견조한 EV향 2170-46시리즈 공급을 물량 중심을 성
장하였고, 폴란드 가동률 개선, 수익성이 견조한 원통형 물량 증가, 북미 ESS 생산성 
개선에 따른 고정비 부담 완화 및 고객사의 일회성 물량 보상금 영향으로 설명. 그러나 
일회성 물량 보상금의 구체적 금액, 반복 발생 여부는 제시하지 않아 불확실성 남겨. 
7,560억원의 영업이익에도 불구하고 영업외손실 9,872억원 발생으로 당기순손실 기
록. 이에는 소형 IT파우치 자산손상 반영되어, 가동률 개선 및 감가상각비 감소를 위해 
Capa를 조정하는 배터리 회사들의 대응 전략이 이번 실적에서 다시 확인

‘30년 ESS 수요 전망들 대비 국내사 Capa 배 이상 높아 
동사는 북미 ESS Capa 확대를 통한 탈중국 수요에 적극 대응하겠다는 전략. 파우치 
라인 추가 전환 및 신규 각형 라인 확보를 통해 북미 ESS Capa를 ‘26년말 50+에서 
‘27년 70+로 제시. 최근 타사에서 ‘26년 미국 ESS 수요를 80~100GWh, 혹은 
130+GWh로, ‘30년 두배 이상, 혹은 240+GWh로 수요 전망에 대해 답변한 바 있음. 
동사 전체 Capa 300GWh(‘25년 연말), SK On 179GWh, SDI 140GWh)임을 감안하
면 ‘30년 약 2배 수요를 가정하면 셀 3사 Capa의 32%에 달하는 수요, 만일 3배 수
요 가정하면 48.5% 수준. 북미 외 시장 가성비 중시 기조 및 중국 LFP 장악(‘25년 
97% M/S)으로 진입장벽 높음을 감안하면 미국 수요 중요. 이를 상쇄하기 위해 EV향 
배터리의 안정적인 공급 지속 필요하며, 이를 위해 동사 원통형은 TESLA의 비미국 시
장 판매량이, 파우치는 시장 자체 축소 지속 여부에 유의할 필요 
상대적으로 가장 견조한 출하량, 그러나 고평가 영역  
셀3사중 EV향 배터리 출하량 및 ESS 탑재에서 현재 가장 견조한 판매량을 보이고 있
으나, AMPC와 일회성 보상금이 영업손익의 상당부분을 견인한다는 점에서 수익성 취
약하다는 단점. 또한 ROE가 (-) 영역 임에도 PBR이 3.x내외로 거래되고 있어 고평가 
지적에서 자유롭기 어려워. 당분간 모회사인 LG화학의 PRS 발행에 따른 단기 오버행 
이슈도 관건. PBR 2.3x(‘25년대비 하락하는 ROE 감안, 20% 할인)적용 목표주가 
290,000원 제시, HOLD 유지. 주식 투자 매력 낮은 상황으로 판단 
 Analyst 정경희 
khchung@ls-sec.co.kr

### Page 2

Company Report / LG에너지솔루션 / 2026. 11.4 
LS Securities Research 2 
EV향 3분기 data  
이차전지에서 EV향 비중은 여전히 가장 높다. 타입별로 보면 3분기 글로벌 EV향 이차전
지에서 각형의 M/S가 83%로 지속 증가하고 있으며, 동사가 생산하는 원통형과 파우치의 
경우 합산 M/S가 17%로 하락하였다. 현재 Wh 기준 동사 EV향 배터리에서 3분기 파우
치가 55%, 원통형이 45%로 파우치 비중이 10% 원통형을 상회한다. 
그림1 LGES 원통형, 파우치 타입 EV 향 분기별 탑재량 추이 
그림2

자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터

그림 2 EV 향 폼팩터 M/S: 각형 확장 vs 원통형/파우치 감소  그림 3 동사 EV 배터리 타입 비중

자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터  자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터

SNE data에 따르면 글로벌 원통형 EV향 수요의 대다수가 TESLA에서 발생하고 있으며, 
‘24~’25년 역성장후 ‘26년 판매량 회복을 나타내는 TESLA 호조로 전년대비 LGES의 EV
향 원통형 판매량은 3분기 증가했다. 반면 파우치의 경우 ‘25년 파우치 탑재 EV 판매 호조
를 나타낸 현대기아차그룹 영향으로 YoY 탑재량이 증가했으나, 올해는 북미 OEM인 GM, 
Ford향 판매가 IRA 일몰로 전년대비 부진함에 따라 3분기 전년대비 25% 감소한 것으로 
나타난다.

그림 9 셀 3 사 분기별 EV 향 탑재량 글로벌 M/S 추이: M/S 감소중

자료: SNE 리서치, LS증권 리서치센터

밸류에이션, 수익성 대비 고평가 영역  
동사는 ‘24년 ROE -4.9%, ‘25년 -5.2%에 이어 ‘26년 -4.4%, ‘27년 -3.4%로 (-) 영역이 
지속되는 것으로 예상한다. ‘24년과 ‘25년 순이익은 (+)지만, 해외 합작회사가 많고 이들 이
익을 연결함에 따라 지배주주순이익은 적자를 기록한 것을 보인다. JV 규모가 축소되고 있
어 이러한 순이익과 지배주주 순이익상의 괴리는 좁혀지겠지만, ROE가 (-)영역 지속을 예
상함에 따라 현재 거래 PBR은 고평가 상황으로 볼 수 있다. 물론 이것은 향후 수익 성장
에 대한 시장 기대가 반영된 것으로 볼 수 있지만, 이차전지 6개사 평균 Consensus 대비 
실제 수익이 ‘23년부터 지속 하향되고 있다는 점, ESS대비 여전히 판매비중이 높은 EV에서 
출하량 정체가 일어나고 있다는 점, 미국 ESS에서 여전히 높은 중국 M/S를 감안하면 이러
한 기대 수준에 대해 다시 검토할 필요가 있다. 이에 더해, 모회사 기준에서는 필요한 과정
이지만 동사 주가 측면에서는 LG화학의 PRS 발행도 단기 오버행으로 당분간 주가에 부담
을 줄 수 있다.

그림 10 셀 3 사, 양∙음극재 3 사 추정치 변화율: ‘23 년부터 추정치 지속 하향 조정

자료: DataGuide, LS증권 리서치센터

미국 ESS가 모든 것을 해결할 수 있나? 
‘25년 글로벌 ESS 시장의 97%를 중국배터리사가 공급했다. LFP 각형이 표준화된 시장인 
것이다. 미국 시장만 중국에 대한 관세 허들, 정책 허들로 한국 배터리사에 기회로 작용한
다. 그러나 ‘26년부터 시작된 FEOC와 관세추가 부담에도 불구, 상반기 미국 ESS 시장의 
76%을 (기타사의 국적을 알 수 없기에 제외하더라도) 중국 셀사가 차지했다. 만일 한국 배
터리 유관회사들이 전망하듯 ‘30년 미국 ESS 시장이 160~200GWh 이상, 240+GWh, 혹
은 240~300GWh가 된다고 해도 3사 전체 Capa의 50%가 채 되지 못한다. EV 수요가 뒷
받침되지 않는다면 미국 ESS만으로는 가동률을 80~90% 이상 올리기 어렵다는 의미다. 특
히 동사는 원통형과 파우치를 생산하고 있으나, 원통형은 북미외 지역 TESLA의 판매에, 
파우치는 올해 출하량이 감소하는 것으로 나타나고 있다. 파우치 고객의 각형 채택 비중 
증가를 감안하면 동사 EV향 배터리 출하량 증가를 확신하기는 어렵다.   
긍정적 기대와 동시에 냉정한 점검이 필요한 시점으로 판단한다.

LS Securities Research 6 
표2 LG 에너지솔루션 3Q26 실적발표 주요 Q&A 
[Q&A]           
Q1-1. 4Q26 실적 전망과 2027 년 큰 방향은? 
  A. 4Q26 ESS 출하량은 북미 수요 대응으로 QoQ 약 40% 증가 예상 
   유럽 고전압 Mid-Ni 파우치 및 원통형 물량도 연말까지 견조한 출하 지속 전망 
   3Q26 가동을 시작한 북미 JV 효과가 더해져 전사 매출은 QoQ 약 10% 성장 가능할 것으로 예상 
   가동률 개선과 사이트 안정적 램프업은 지속 추진하나 3Q26 일회성 요인 소멸의 기저효과 존재 
   4Q26 혼다 JV ESS CAPA 추가 가동 준비에 따른 초기 고정비 부담으로 수익성 일부 둔화 불가피

2027 년 사업계획은 수립 중이며 전방 수요 변수로 구체적 가이던스 제시는 어려운 단계 
    ESS: 북미 확보 수주 기반으로 유의미한 물량 성장 지속 기대 
    소형: 전략 고객향 EV 원통형과 46 시리즈 신규 애플리케이션 판매로 안정적 성장 예상 
    EV 파우치: 중저가 제품 물량과 북미 EV 향 생산품이 더해져 성장 예상 
   전 사업부 매출 성장 기대하나 핵심 제품 경쟁력·기존 CAPA 효율화·가동률 극대화·원가 개선에 집중 
   2027 년 매출 성장률·영업이익률 등 구체적 수치는 제시 X

Q1-2. 북미 PFE 요건 충족을 위한 LFP 공급망 전략과 중국 관련 주력 양극재 공급사 대응은? 
  A. 미국 생산세액공제 확보를 위해 직접재료비 내 PFE 소재 비중 요건을 충족하는 공급망 구축 필요 
   회사 설명상 PFE 소재 허용 비중은 2026 년 40%에서 2027 년 35% 이하로 강화. Non-PFE 비중과 혼동 유의 
   소재별 소싱 전략을 구체화해 준비 중이며 요건 충족은 차질 없이 진행될 것으로 판단 
   재료비 비중이 큰 양극재는 최근 계약한 국내 업체 외 복수 Non-PFE 업체 조달 옵션 확보

음극재·분리막·전해액도 기존 Non-PFE 공급망에 더해 신규 업체 적극 발굴 
   기존 원가 중심 최적화에서 정책·지정학 리스크를 고려한 공급망 안정성 강화로 전략 확대 
   협력사의 원가·기술·물량 대응력을 종합 평가하고 장기적으로 현지 협력사 발굴·현지화 추진 
   질문한 중국 관련 주력 공급사의 개별 지위·조달 비중에 대한 직접적인 상세 답변 X

Q2-1. ESS 사업의 수익성 전망 및 개선 방안은? 
  A. EV→ESS 라인 전환으로 수요에 맞는 생산체계를 구축하고 북미 고객 적기 공급에 주력 
   다수 사이트 동시 램프업에 어려움이 있었고 고정비 절감 노력에도 수익성 개선 속도는 시장 기대보다 느렸음 
   2026 년 말 50GWh 목표 셀 라인 램프업은 마무리 국면. Link CAPA 도 3Q26 에 이어 4Q26 추가 보강 예정 
   CAPA 안정화와 점진적인 고정비 부담 완화 기대 
   확보 CAPA 효율화, 소재 소싱 다변화로 원재료비·물류비 절감에 집중 
   AI 데이터센터 및 SI 프로젝트 비중 확대를 통한 고객 포트폴리오 최적화 추진 
   셀·시스템 고용량화로 원가 경쟁력을 높여 구조적 수익성 개선 추진 
   구체적 ESS 영업이익률 목표나 개선 폭은 제시 X

Q2-2. 북미 ESS 추가 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 빠른 시장 성장 및 Non-PFE 에 우호적인 정책으로 북미 현지 생산 셀 수요 급증 
   현재 계획된 CAPA 기준으로 2028 년까지 물량은 대부분 확정. 그 이후 물량 중심으로 고객 수주 협의 진행 
   기존 전력망·유틸리티 고객, 이들을 통한 AI 데이터센터 BESS 수요, 하이퍼스케일러 BTM 직접 구매 수요 포함 
   예상 수요가 공급 가능 CAPA 를 초과해 전략적으로 중요한 프로젝트 중심으로 미래 잔여 CAPA 운영 계획 
   추가 고객명·신규 계약량 등은 제시하지 않음. 예상 수요와 확정 수주를 구분

Q3-1. 46 시리즈의 구체적인 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 기존 OEM 부터 EV 스타트업까지 이미 440GWh 이상 물량 확보 
   추가로 다수 글로벌 OEM 과 협의 중이며 가까운 시일 내 의미 있는 규모의 수주 성과 기대 
   제품 경쟁력·지역별 생산 역량으로 46 시리즈 선도 입지를 강화하고 고객 포트폴리오 확대 계획 
   440GWh 는 확보 물량이며 연간 판매량이나 생산 CAPA 가 아님. 추가 계약 규모·고객명은 미공개

### Page 7

Company Report / LG에너지솔루션 / 2026. 11.4 
LS Securities Research 7

Q3-2. Tabless 2170 의 경쟁력과 수주·신규 시장 대응 현황은? 
  A. 기존 제품 대비 낮은 발열·저항으로 안전성 및 고출력에 유리해 HEV·BBU·로봇 등 다양한 용도에 적합 
   오창에서 전동공구용 제품은 이미 양산 중  
   BBU 향 LFP 2170 은 2Q27 양산 목표로 준비 중 
   HEV 향은 2027 년 말 애리조나에서 양산 계획  
   LFP 2170 은 NCM 대비 열안전성 테스트에서 우수한 성능을 보여 고객의 긍정적 피드백 확보 
   고객 니즈·수요에 적극 대응해 신시장 경쟁력 확보 추진. 수주 규모 등 정량 정보는 제시하지 않음

Q4-1. 재무 건전성 개선 방안과 2027 년 자금 조달 계획은? 
  A. 선제 CAPA 투자 자금은 저리 정책자금 장기차입 및 원화·외화 회사채로 안정적으로 조달해 왔음 
   주요 투자는 대부분 집행됐고 CAPEX 는 2025 년을 기점으로 유의미하게 감소 중 
   향후 영업현금흐름 확대분을 차입금 상환에 우선 활용, 자산 유동화를 통한 추가 재원 확보도 병행 
   차입금·금융비용 부담을 축소해 재무지표 개선 및 건전성 확보 추진 
   2026 년 본사 추가 차입 계획은 없으며 해외법인은 기존 약정 차입금 일부 추가 인출 예정 
   2027 년은 기존 차입금 차환·운전자금 범위에서 최소한의 자금 조달 계획 
   영업현금 확대·투자 부담 완화에 따른 잉여 재원으로 차입금 감축 기조 지속. 구체적 조달 규모는 미제시

Q4-2. 유럽 IAA 정책 방향에 대응한 중장기 점유율 확보 전략은? 
  A. IAA 는 아직 최종안 미확정이나 수정 권고안상 외국인 직접투자·현지화 요건 강화 예상 
   배터리뿐 아니라 주요 소재의 현지 생산 역량 중요성이 확대될 전망 
   유럽 OEM 의 공급망 다변화로 현지 생산 체계를 갖춘 비중국 업체 소싱 니즈 확대, 경쟁 환경은 우호적 판단 
   폴란드 현지 양산 및 소재·부품 공급망을 이미 구축해 현지 배터리 소싱 수요에 신속 대응 가능 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 에 대한 유럽 고객 관심 증가. 폴란드 감가상각비 감소도 가격 경쟁력 확보에 기여 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 외 LFP·CTP 등 다양한 제품·솔루션으로 신규 수주를 확보해 유럽 점유율 확대 추진

자료: LS증권 리서치센터

## Document 314: Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-18)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ cost of living front and centre (2026-11-18).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: cost of living front and centre
By Ryan Wells, Elliot Clarke & Illiana Jain
09:49 November 13 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment Index fell 2.7% to 78.2 in November, its weakest
reading since late-2022 and a level historically associated with periods of severe economic stress.
Renewed cost-of-living and interest rate pressures were front and centre following another lift in

inflation, including sharply higher petrol prices, and the RBA’s November rate increase. Mortgage
holders are feeling the squeeze most acutely, with sentiment across this group falling to a record low.

### Page 2

Consumers have become noticeably more downbeat on the near-term economic outlook – the relevant
sub-index dropping 10.2% to be 22% below its long-run average. Job-loss fears also continue to edge
up.
Businesses are similarly troubled. The latest NAB business survey showed confidence slipped further
to –15 in October as conditions held near their weakest level since the pandemic. Of concern for the
outlook, forward orders fell to –12, also the weakest read since the pandemic, and capital expenditure

intentions deteriorated. Purchase costs continue to grow materially faster than selling prices, further
compressing already tight margins.
Housing provides a clear example of how caution and tighter financial conditions can jolt activity. The
value of new dwelling finance approvals plunged 15% in Q3, the largest quarterly decline in the series’
history, taking approvals 25% lower year-to-date. Investor lending bore the brunt of the adjustment,
falling 30% over the quarter as higher rates combined with the housing tax changes announced in May.

Owner-occupier lending also weakened, however, with first-home buyers proving particularly sensitive
to the deterioration in affordability and market conditions.
Taken together, the data points to a further loss of momentum across the domestic economy,
consistent with Westpac’s expectation for subdued growth and concern over downside activity risks.
Meanwhile, in the US, October’s headline CPI met our expectations but at 0.4% was a touch above the

consensus view. Energy and food both contributed meaningfully to the result, but core inflation was
also marginally stronger than forecast, +0.3%. Core goods inflation has accelerated over the past four
months to average a gain of 0.2% as businesses look to pass tariffs, tech and energy input cost
inflation through. Shelter meanwhile continued to rise at an above-average pace, gaining 0.3% in
October.
The market impact of the October CPI report was accentuated by hawkish remarks from Fed Governor

Waller prior to the release. He expressed confidence in economic activity and the labour market while
showing significant concern over the strength and persistence of inflation, focusing on the passthrough
of energy and technology input inflation. His conclusion for policy was clear: "if, as I expect, the
monthly rate of inflation improves in October, I am leaning toward supporting an increase in our policy
rate of 25 basis points at the FOMC's December meeting"; but "if the October CPI inflation data out

later today are similar to the past two monthly readings—with core CPI inflation around 0.3 percent or
even higher—then a stronger policy move may be needed".
Regional Fed Presidents Goolsbee, Logan and Hammack also kept the focus on inflation risks and
stated or implied the FOMC needs to act. Market participants have responded to this week’s updates,
pricing a 25bp hike in for the December and January meetings, and a cumulative 3.5 hikes by
September. We believe this pricing overstates the need for policy tightening given core inflation ex

shelter remains relatively benign at 2.4% on a six-month annualised basis and the labour market is
balanced; but it will take time for price concerns to subside.
While we agree that the US economy is strong in aggregate, US households are under pressure from
the cost of living, and their investment decision making is constrained by borrowing costs. Highlighting

### Page 3

Related articles
this, US existing home sales fell 5.5% in October after a downwardly revised 1.0% gain in September,
leaving the level of sales below the cycle lows of 2009 and 2010. Our November Market Outlook will
provide a full update on our expectations for the US and the world when released on Westpac IQ this
afternoon.

## Document 319: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

## Document 348: Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FOLLOWING THE OIL SHOCK, EL NIÑO THREATENS TO HURT MACROECONOMIC RESILIENCE IN SUB-SAHARAN
AFRICA FURTHER
Lucas Plé
Seven months after the start of the war in the Middle East, which has led to a sharp rise in oil prices, sub-Saharan Africa is holding up well
overall. The deterioration in external accounts and the rise in inflation are more contained than in 2022, during the last oil shock. However,
this resilience is fragile and is eroding as the conflict drags on. Furthermore, an exceptional El Niño event could significantly worsen the

macroeconomic environment in 2027 1.
Economic growth remains robust
For countries with available data, GDP growth held up well in the first half of 2026 2. In mid-October, the IMF revised its regional forecast for
the full year upwards to 4.5% (+0.2 percentage points compared with April), in line with the performance for 2025.
1 El Niño 2026–2027: multiple pressures on prices and supplies
2 1.4% year-on-year (y/y) in South Africa, 4.2% in Nigeria, 5.1% in Kenya, 7% in Angola, 7.3% in Ghana.

Sub-Saharan Africa is highly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 21 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A mitigated shock on terms of trade
High prices for export commodities (gold, copper, platinum) have helped to cushion the impact on terms of trade and the external
accounts of hydrocarbon-importing countries. For the 10 countries in our sample (see heatmap), the depreciation of their currencies
against the US dollar since last February has been moderate overall (2.3% on average).

Inflation has started to rise again, but it is still being contained so far
In the ten countries in our sample, although inflation is rising again, it is lower year-on-year for the first eight months of 2026 than it was
for the same period in 2022. This is due to:
1. Monetary policies that were still restrictive before the conflict started;
2. Fuel subsidies;
3.   Macroeconomic reforms implemented in recent years (a return to orthodox monetary policy in Nigeria, and efforts towards fiscal

consolidation in many countries);
4. Moderate currency depreciation since February.
As a result, few central banks have tightened their monetary policy since February (South Africa, Uganda and Tanzania).
However, this resilience is still fragile…
On the one hand, governments that have chosen to cap fuel price rises are seeing their public finances deteriorate. In Senegal, energy
subsidies will go above 3% of GDP in 2026, which is unsustainable. On the other hand, the region’s external accounts are exposed to

volatile global commodity prices and tighter international financing conditions.
… and the macroeconomic environment could quickly worsen in 2027, due to an exceptional El Niño event
The region is particularly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel. Given agriculture’s high share in its GDP,
sub-Saharan Africa is more exposed to downside risks to growth than Latin America, Asia or Central Europe (1 st column of the heatmap).

It is also more exposed than other regions of the world to the inflationary effect of rising food prices, given their higher share in the
consumer basket (2 nd column). Finally, even though its dependence on food imports is lower than in Asia and Europe (3 rd column), the
region is highly vulnerable to rising international food commodity prices due to its fragile external accounts. For several countries in our
sample, foreign exchange reserves cover less than four months’ worth of imports of goods and services (Uganda, Tanzania, Ghana and

Zambia), leaving little room for manoeuvre for absorbing a further external shock.
In addition to shocks via the agricultural commodities channel, other localised risks must be considered. In Southern Africa, El Niño is
expected to intensify droughts and threaten electricity generation as a result, particularly in Zambia (where hydropower accounts
for nearly 80% of domestic electricity generation). In East Africa, the increased flooding that El Niño is expected to cause will threaten

infrastructure – particularly logistics infrastructure – which is already less efficient than the global average 3. These two sources of
vulnerability could also increase pressure on growth, prices and the external accounts.
In conclusion, if oil prices remain high and El Niño is exceptionally severe, the region will struggle to absorb this double shock in 2027.
Despite their status as net exporters of hydrocarbons, Nigeria and Angola are also exposed to a deterioration in the macroeconomic

environment in 2027. Only South Africa appears to be better positioned to absorb the shock.
lucas.ple@bnpparibas.com
3 See the World Bank’s Logistics Performance Index.

### Page 3

The bank for
a changing
world

teness or fitness for any particular purpose of such information and such information may not

## Document 394: NZ labour market review chartpack, Q3 2026 (2026-11-18)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ labour market review chartpack, Q3 2026 (2026-11-18).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期

### Page 1

LABOUR MARKET CHARTPACK
4 Nov 2026

### Page 2

Westpac New Zealand Economics
2
Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz      Connect with us
Key points – September quarter 2026
• The September quarter surveys pointed to ongoing softness in labour market conditions. The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing an
almost five-year run of increases. The unemployment rate is now at its highest level since September 2015.
• There were mixed messages in terms of jobs growth: the Household Labour Force Survey reported a 0.1% fall in employment, while the employer-

focused Quarterly Employment Survey suggested a modest rise. The Monthly Employment Indicator had also pointed to a small rise in jobs.
• The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in the participation rate. These co-movements essentially reversed the surprisingly large
increases that we saw in the June quarter, which we had suggested at the time were more likely due to sampling error in the survey rather than a
genuine surge.
• We generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate, which is less vulnerable to sampling errors – and the message here is one of softness. Our

best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not fast enough to absorb the growth in the population.
• While the economy has returned to growth over the last year or so, hiring activity remains weak. This may be an ongoing legacy of employment trends in
previous years, when firms were relatively reluctant to lay people off during the slowdown, which then potentially left them overstaffed even as activity
started to pick up again. It’s not clear how long this effect can persist, though.

• Wage growth remains muted. Although cost-of-living pressures have increased on workers, the existing degree of spare capacity in the labour market
doesn’t give them much leverage to negotiate larger pay increases.
• Overall, we judge these results to be weaker than the RBNZ expected, in particular the rise in the unemployment rate. This data supports the views of
those on the policy committee who were less convinced in October about the potential for second-round inflation pressures in the economy.

### Page 3

Westpac New Zealand Economics
3
Employment
• The number of people employed fell by 0.1% in the September quarter, according to the
Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS). Previous quarters were revised down a little,
reflecting downward revisions to the working-age population estimates. Annual employment
growth slowed from 0.9% to 0.7%.
• This was substantially softer than our forecast of a 0.5% rise for the quarter. Our forecast
was based on the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI), a comprehensive measure that is

drawn from income tax data. While there are some differences in coverage for these two
measures, any significant divergence between the two is most likely to reflect sampling error
in the HLFS.
• Meanwhile, the employer-focused Quarterly Employment Survey (QES) recorded a 0.1% rise
in the number of filled jobs and a 0.6% rise in hours paid. (This survey is of course vulnerable
to sampling error as well.)
• We’re most inclined towards the MEI measure of employment, given the breadth of its

coverage. But our best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not
yet fast enough to absorb the growth in the adult population – hence the further rise in the
unemployment rate.

### Page 4

Westpac New Zealand Economics
4
Labour force participation
• The surprising fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a drop in the participation rate
from 70.6% to 70.4%.
• These co-movements are effectively a reversal of the surprisingly large increases that we
saw in the June quarter, which we noted at the time were suggestive of sampling error in the
survey. Any such errors largely wash out when it comes to the unemployment rate, however,
which is why we recommend focusing on this measure.

• Setting aside the usual quarterly volatility, labour force participation remains caught
between two competing forces. One is cyclical weakness, especially among younger people,
who are finding it difficult to break into a “low-fire, low-hire” labour market. The teen
unemployment rate of around 25% is a record high going back to the start of the survey
in 1986.
• The other force is rising participation among the over-60s (who also make up a growing

share of the population). Kiwis have had a growing propensity to work beyond the NZ Super
age, as there is no penalty for doing so. It’s unclear to what extent this can be considered
an increase in the pool of available workers: older workers tend to have low mobility
between jobs, and if they find themselves out of work are more likely to leave the labour
force altogether.

### Page 5

Westpac New Zealand Economics
5
Unemployment and underutilisation
• The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7% in the September quarter. This was the
highest rate since September 2015.
• While there are other indicators of spare capacity in the labour market, we’re not convinced
that there is additional information to draw from them this time.
• The broader underutilisation measure, which includes discouraged jobseekers and part-
time workers wanting more hours, was steady at 13.8% after having risen sharply from

12.9% last quarter. This measure is both higher (by definition) and more variable than the
unemployment rate, but tells a similar story in that it’s around an 11-year high.
• There was a marked 1.6% fall in the number of hours worked (although ‘usual’ hours were
down just 0.4%). However, we suspect this was largely driven by Matariki – a floating holiday
that won’t be captured in the seasonal adjustment.
• This may also account for the 2.5% rise in part-time employment, against a 0.7% fall in

full-time employment. Some workers may have been pushed into the part-time category
(less than 30 hours a week) simply by virtue of only working four days during the
survey week.

### Page 6

Westpac New Zealand Economics
6
Hiring
• Business confidence surveys have shown a lift in hiring intentions in recent months, as firms
have shaken off the initial shock of the Iran conflict and the surge in fuel prices.
• However, those stated intentions aren’t obviously turning into reality yet. Job advertisements
were down about 1% for the September quarter in seasonally adjusted terms, and they
remain well below pre-Covid levels. Job ads will capture ‘churn’ within existing roles as well

as new job creation, but both of these would be associated with a strong labour market.
• With the working-age population growing at the same time, the result was a modest fall in
the vacancy rate, one of the RBNZ’s highest-ranked measures of labour market tightness.
• We estimate that there are around 1.9 people unemployed for every job vacancy, up from
around 1.8 people this time last year.
• Firms have told us that they were reluctant to lay off workers during the economic downturn

in 2023-24, as they were wary of the extreme labour shortages that had developed in the
previous boom years. As a result, they may find themselves still overstaffed even as demand
has started to pick up again, and haven’t needed to return to hiring yet.

### Page 7

Westpac New Zealand Economics
7
Population and migration
• The working-age population grew by around 1.0% over the last year, compared to a peak of
3.3% growth over 2023. Jobs growth (based on the less volatile MEI measure) is still lagging
behind population growth on an annual basis.
• The pool of potential workers is generally becoming older, with the Baby Boomers
increasingly reaching pension age and beyond, and with migrant arrivals tending to be an

older cohort than we saw pre-Covid – more in their 30s and 40s than their 20s.
• Net migration flows have started to pick up from their lows since late 2025. Departures of
New Zealanders have come down from their highs, though they remain above pre-Covid
levels, as the relative appeal of the Australian jobs market has narrowed.
• Foreign arrivals have picked up to some degree, although the growth appears to have been
mostly driven by students rather than those on work visas.

### Page 8

Westpac New Zealand Economics
8
Sectoral and regional trends
• Jobs growth has been relatively strong in government-connected sectors such as healthcare
and education, as well as in local and central government administration.
• Mining and utilities have seen strong growth in percentage terms, but neither of these
sectors are big employers in absolute terms.
• The construction sector is now starting to see a lift in employment, after a steep decline

over 2024-25. Residential building consents have risen strongly over the last year or so,
pointing to a growing pipeline of work for the homebuilding industry.
• Sectors that are connected to international tourism, such as transport and recreational
services, had previously seen strong employment growth, but this appears to have flattened
out in recent months.
• Employment trends have generally been more positive in the South Island than the North,

which likely reflects the greater influence of the agricultural sector – while not a large
employer in itself, farmers have benefited from high export prices and their spending has
flowed into other sectors.

### Page 9

Westpac New Zealand Economics
9
Wages
• The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September quarter. Annual growth
remained around the lowest since 2021.
• While the overall result was in line with our forecast, the details were a little weaker than
we expected, with less contribution from private sector wages (up 0.5%) and a surprisingly
large 0.7% increase in the public sector.
• The distribution of pay rates has returned to pre-Covid trends. Only 44% of roles saw a

pay rise over the last year; of those roles that did see an increase, the average size has
converged on the 2-3% range.
• The LCI is not a measure of what workers actually receive – it reflects the cost to businesses
for a given unit of labour – and hence shouldn’t be compared to the inflation rate. A more
suitable measure is the analytical unadjusted LCI, which includes pay rises that are related
to productivity gains. This measure slowed from 3.1% to 2.9% on an annual basis, the lowest

since March 2021.
• There was a marked pickup in the proportion of firms reporting cost-of-living adjustments as
the main reason for pay increases. However, the size of those increases varied greatly, with
many of them below the rate of CPI inflation.
• This seems to reflect a shift in the justification for pay rises, rather than an overall increase in
wage pressures – firms are more likely to cite cost-of-living adjustments because they’re less
likely to cite demand-side pressures.

### Page 10

Westpac New Zealand Economics
10
International comparisons
• New Zealand’s unemployment rate is the 18th lowest in the OECD (out of 38 countries). This
ranking has been broadly unchanged over the last year or so.
• Notably, New Zealand’s unemployment rate is substantially above Australia’s, whereas
historically it has been lower on average. This shift in relative job opportunities has been a
key attractor for New Zealanders over the last couple of years – as it was in the early 2010s

when the Australian economy was benefiting from a boom in mining construction.
• However, that gap between unemployment rates is no longer widening, with the Australian
jobs market starting to soften this year as the RBA has raised interest rates to rein in inflation
(which was already running too high before the Iran conflict and the surge in oil prices). We
expect Australia’s unemployment to rise further over the year ahead.
• New Zealand has the 7th highest employment rate in the OECD (down from 6th last quarter).

We tend to have high rates of labour force participation for 15-64 year olds, and particularly
high participation for the over-65s, as the lack of means testing for NZ Superannuation
means there is no financial disincentive to working beyond pension age.

## Document 411: QE速報：2026年7-9月期の実質ＧＤＰは前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）－民間消費の落ち込みを主因として4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長

Source PDF: all report pdf/QE速報：2026年7-9月期の実質ＧＤＰは前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）－民間消費の落ち込みを主因として4四半期ぶりのマイナス成長.pdf

実質ＧＤＰ成長率の推移
民間最終消費支出 民間企業設備 その他民需 公需 外需
（資料）内閣府経済社会総合研究所「四半期別ＧＤＰ速報」
( )内は年率換算値

１. 2026年 7-9月期の実質 GDPは、前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）と 4四半期ぶりのマイナ
ス成長となった（当研究所予測 10月 30日：前期比▲0.1％、年率▲0.3％）。 
２. 物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下や天候不順の影響で民間消費が前期比
▲0.3％の減少となったことがマイナス成長の主因である。一方、高水準の企業収益を背
景に設備投資は前期比 1.6％の高い伸びとなった。 
３. 外需寄与度は前期比▲0.6％（前期比年率▲2.4％）と 3四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。
財貨・サービスの輸出が前期比▲0.2％と減少する一方、代替調達の進展に伴う原油輸入
量の急増を主因として財貨・サービスの輸入が同 2.5％の増加となった。 
４. 原油の代替調達に伴う輸入増加による成長率の押し下げ（前期比寄与度▲0.5％）は、国
家備蓄の放出がほぼなくなったことによる公的在庫変動のプラス寄与（同 0.5％）によっ
てほぼ相殺されている。両者を合わせた成長率への影響はほぼ中立となった。 
５. マイナス成長の主因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響が大きい
が、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が高い。
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むこ
となどから、前期比年率 0％台半ばとなり、マイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにと
どまると予想している。 
ニッセイ基礎研究所 2026-11-16

### Page 3

＜需要項目別の動き＞ 
民間消費は、物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下や天候不順の影響で、前期比▲0.3％と
2四半期連続で減少した。2026年 4-6月期が前期比 0.0％から同▲0.3％へ下方修正されたため、民
間消費の減少は 2四半期連続となった。 
3月末の自動車税の環境性能割廃止に伴う自動車販売の大幅増加や、2027年 4月の省エネ基準引
き上げを見越した駆け込み需要によるエアコン販売の好調により、4-6月期に大幅に増加した耐久
財が、その反動で大きく落ち込んだことも消費の下押し要因となった。 
実質家計消費の内訳を形態別にみると、外食、旅行、宿泊などのサービスは前期比 0.1％と小幅
な増加となったが、自動車、エアコンなどの耐久財が前期比▲3.5％の減少となったほか、値上げが
続く食料品などの非耐久財が同▲0.2％、被服・履物、家具などの半耐久財が同▲1.4％の減少とな
った。 
名目雇用者報酬は前年比 5.5％と、4-6月期の同 5.3％から伸びが高まった。実質雇用者報酬は前
年比 2.5％（4-6月期：同 2.6％）と 10四半期連続で増加した。

住宅投資は前期比▲1.1％と 2四半期連続で減少した。新設住宅着工戸数（季節調整済・年率換算
値）は、建築物省エネ法・建築基準法改正前の駆け込み需要の反動で、2025年 1-3月期の 86.7万
戸から 4-6月期に 62.0万戸へ急減した後、7-9月が 71.9万戸と持ち直し、その後は概ね 70万戸台
半ばで推移している。GDP統計の住宅投資は住宅価格上昇の影響もあり弱めの動きとなっている。

設備投資は前期比 1.6％と 3四半期ぶりに増加した。設備投資が高い伸びとなった一因は、4-6月
期に特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研究開発投資の減少によって落ち込んだ反動である。ただ、その影響
を除いても、高水準の企業収益を背景に、人手不足に対応した省力化投資、デジタル化に向けた情
報関連投資、AI・半導体関連などの成長分野への投資、Eコマースの拡大に伴う建設投資などを中
心に、回復基調が続いている。

公的需要は前期比 1.8％と 2四半期ぶりに増加した。高校授業料無償化や公立小学校の給食費無
償化に伴う家計消費支出の減少分が計上され、4-6月期に前期比 1.5％の高い伸びとなった政府消費
がその反動で同▲0.0％と減少し、公的固定資本形成は前期比▲1.1％と 2四半期連続で減少した。
一方、原油輸入量の急減を受けた国家備蓄原油の放出により前期比・寄与度▲0.5％の大幅マイナス
となった公的在庫変動が、備蓄の放出がほぼなくなったことで、プラス寄与（同 0.5％）に転じた
ため、公的需要全体では前期比 1.8％の増加となった。

外需寄与度は前期比▲0.6％（前期比年率▲2.4％）と 3四半期ぶりのマイナスとなった。財貨・
サービスの輸出は前期比▲0.2％と 4四半期ぶりに減少した。半導体製造装置などの資本財輸出が好
調だったことから、財輸出は同 2.4％と増加したが、4-6月期に大幅に増加した研究開発サービスの

### Page 4

（2026年度後半は低空飛行が続く見通し） 
2026年 7-9月期のマイナス成長の主因となった民間消費の減少は、天候不順による一時的な影響
が大きい。ただし、こうした影響が解消されても、物価上昇の加速が消費の回復を妨げる可能性が
高い。消費者物価上昇率は 2026年 9月に 9ヵ月ぶりの 2％台となったが、原材料価格の上昇を耐久
財や日用品などの販売価格に転嫁する動きが今後さらに広がることから、2026年度末にかけて 3％
台まで高まる公算が大きい。物価上昇率の高まりによる実質購買力の低下を背景に 2026年度後半
の民間消費は弱めの動きが続くことが予想される。 
現時点では、2026年 10-12月期はプラス成長に復帰するものの、民間消費が伸び悩むことなどか
ら、前期比年率 0％台半ばとなり、マイナス成長の後としては低めの伸びにとどまると予想してい
る。続く 2027年 1-3月期は、物価上昇率が 3％台まで高まることに加え、飲食料品の消費税率引き
下げを控えた買い控えも影響し、民間消費が減少する可能性が高く、実質 GDP成長率は前期比年
率 0％台前半にとどまるだろう。2026年度後半の日本経済は物価高による民間消費の低迷を主因と
して、低空飛行が続くことが予想される。

本資料記載のデータは各種の情報源から入手・加工したものであり、その正確性と完全性を保証するものではありません。 
また、本資料は情報提供が目的であり、記載の意見や予測は、いかなる契約の締結や解約を勧誘するものではありません。

⼩型新興雲將提早被淘汰出局，我們認為 SpaceXSI, Nebius, 及 CoreWeave
這三家新興雲要完成 2027 年營收倍增，應該不難。
Source: The companies, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs

新興 AI 推理晶⽚，測試設備，光纖雷射營收倍增受惠：跟 AMD 在 AI 推理⽅⾯
密切合作的  Cerebras System 系統晶⽚公司，也是採⽤台積電  System on

設備需求。 Applied Optoelectronics 及 AXT 受惠於 AI 數據算⼒中⼼的光纖
互聯雷射光需求的爆發。 Nvidia 因 CEO Jensen Huang 離職，雖然短期 Vera

Adj EPS turning into positive from earlier losses

不同於台灣投資⼈沒有  non-GAAP 或調整後財報數字作為參考，美國會計準

## Document 437: Top of Mind_ Higher bond yields_ how concerning_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Top of Mind_ Higher bond yields_ how concerning_.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 31
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Global bond yields have risen to multi-decade highs, yet growth and markets have
remained remarkably resilient. But with some pressures beginning to emerge (e.g. in
European sovereign bond markets), can this resilience last, or will more vulnerabilities
be revealed? We ask the IMF’s Tobias Adrian, Moody’s Analytics’ Mark Zandi, and GS
economists. Adrian is relatively sanguine about the economy’s resilience, though
notes downside risks, while GS economists expect only a modest growth drag from

higher rates, but a more consequential one if yields remain near current or higher
levels. Zandi is generally more concerned, describing the US economy as vulnerable
rather than resilient, and sees one-in-three odds of the economy going off the rails.
Meanwhile, GS strategists and analysts are not especially concerned about banks or
markets more broadly, but warn that further increases in yields or rate volatility could
test risk assets. Key to watch: whether the increasingly interconnected and leveraged

AI investment boom can continue to withstand higher rates.
Tobias Adrian, Special Advisor and former Director of Monetary and Capital Markets, IMF
“The global economy has demonstrated considerable resilience in the face of multiple severe shocks, and I would not be
surprised if it continues to shrug off higher yields, provided they do not keep rising sharply in a disorderly fashion. At the same
time, I do worry about downside risks.”
Mark Zandi, Chief Economist, Moody’s Analytics
